Key Design Upgrades

🎨 New Visual Identity


  • Dark navy + forest green + sage palette — premium, trustworthy, global
  • Playfair Display (serif) for headings + Plus Jakarta Sans for body — luxury editorial feel
  • Floating glowing orbs, grid overlays, and radial gradients in the hero

🔥 Section-by-Section Overhaul


| Section | What Changed |
|---|---|
| Navbar | Transparent on hero → frosted glass on scroll |
| Hero | Full-screen dark with destination photo cards grid + live stats |
| Marquee | Animated ticker with all destinations |
| Programs | Hover-lift cards with image badges |
| Why Choose | Animated top-bar reveal cards |
| Stats | Dark band with glow gradients |
| Partners | Auto-scroll logo marquee with fade edges |
| Testimonials | Clean 3-column grid with star ratings |
| CTA Banner | Cinematic dark overlay with editorial layout |
| FAQ | Smooth accordion with rotating + icon |
| Footer | 4-column with newsletter signup |

✅ All Content Preserved


Every section from the original — programs, stats, testimonials, FAQ, facilitators — is kept and elevated.

    Session Stage #10 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    Pill Filters An elegant row of pills ("All Moments", "University Campuses", "Industry Visits") built to match the design system

    not working assign something randomly if needed

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Hit F5! The Pill Filters on the Gallery page are now fully interactive.

    I’ve mapped out the logic so that:

  • 1. When you click a filter pill (like "Industry Visits"), the active button state slides over to it smoothly.

  • 2. The grid instantly filters the photos, using a crisp scale-down/fade-out animation to hide the non-matching items.

  • 3. The remaining matching images automatically seamlessly reflow into the CSS masonry layout, closing the empty gaps dynamically.

    • Because we didn't have exact categorization metadata for each photo from the staging server, I built a smart looping distributor. As the photos render into the grid, it pseudo-randomly assigns an even mixture of "University", "Industry", and "Culture" tags to the photos so you can immediately see and present how the filtering functionality behaves!

    Session Stage #14 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    remove testi-watermark and Navigation is not showing so please recode this

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Everything is clean now. Here's what I fixed:

    3 issues resolved:

  • 1. Broken HTML — The header div was mangled (<div class="testimonial <div class="testi-slider-wrapper). I rewrote it cleanly with the proper header ("Student Stories / What Our Students Say") as a separate <div> above the slider wrapper.

  • 2. Navigation arrows not showing — Two problems:

    • The arrows were positioned at left: -70px / right: -70px, pushing them completely outside the visible area. Now they're at left: -24px / right: -24px (slightly overlapping the container edge) with a fallback to 8px inset on narrower screens.
    • The old JS used scrollBy() on a scroll-based approach which didn't work reliably. I rewrote it as a proper transform-based slider — the track moves via translateX(), which is more reliable and gives smooth CSS-animated transitions.

  • 3. Watermark removed — All <div class="testi-watermark">"</div> elements removed from every card, and the .testi-watermark CSS rule deleted entirely.

    • 👉 Hard-refresh http://127.0.0.1:3000/#testimonials to see the working slider with arrows!

     Loading Button Animation — The submit button changes to 'Sending...' and disables itself during transit to prevent double-submits.
    Beautiful Success State — Instead of a native browser alert popup, the form fields slide away and present a beautiful centered 'Message Sent' checkmark banner on success.
     Error Safe-Guard — Includes a 'Failed' banner with a 'Try Again' button if network issues occur, preventing form loss.
    Spam Prevention — Integrated a hidden botcheck honeypot checkbox to automatically filter spam bots without using ugly Captchas.
     Form Serializations — Added matching name attributes (name, email, phone, custom_subject, message) to all fields so the entries parse correctly in Web3Forms.

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    The implementation in contact.html is complete. Now I will write the explanation and guide.Yes, we can absolutely set it up so that when a user submits the form, it:

  • 1. Directly logs the data into a Google Sheet (which you can view/edit in Microsoft Excel anytime or download as an .xlsx file).

  • 2. Sends an email notification using your Gmail account to your inbox automatically!

    • This is the standard and completely free serverless way to do it on a static HTML website.

    ---

    📋 Setup Instructions:

    #### 1. Set up the Google Sheet:

  • 1. Create a new Google Sheet.

  • 2. In the first row, add these exact column headers (case-sensitive):

    • timestamp
     name
    email
     phone
    custom_subject
     message

    #### 2. Create the Google Apps Script:

  • 1. In your Google Sheet menu, click Extensions ➔ Apps Script.

  • 2. Delete any default code and paste the following script:

    • var sheetName = 'Sheet1'; // Make sure this matches your sheet tab name
        var scriptProp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
        function initialSetup () {
          var activeSpreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
          scriptProp.setProperty('key', activeSpreadsheet.getId());
        }
        function doPost (e) {
          var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
          lock.tryLock(10000); // Wait up to 10s for other writes to finish
          try {
            var doc = SpreadsheetApp.openById(scriptProp.getProperty('key'));
            var sheet = doc.getSheetByName(sheetName);
            var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
            var nextRow = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
            var newRow = headers.map(function(header) {
              return header === 'timestamp' ? new Date() : e.parameter[header];
            });
            sheet.getRange(nextRow, 1, 1, newRow.length).setValues([newRow]);
            // Send Email via your Gmail:
            MailApp.sendEmail({
              to: "info@vijigishu.co.in", // Replace with your target inbox email
     
    <truncated 397 bytes>
    m_subject'] + "<br>" +
                        "<b>Message:</b> " + e.parameter['message']
            });
            return ContentService
              .createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ 'result': 'success', 'row': nextRow }))
              .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
          }
          catch (err) {
            return ContentService
              .createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ 'result': 'error', 'error': err }))
              .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
          }
          finally {
            lock.releaseLock();
          }
        }

  • 3. Click the Save icon (disk symbol).

  • 4. Run the initialSetup function once by choosing it from the dropdown at the top and clicking Run. (It will ask for permissions to access Sheets & Gmail; accept them).

    • #### 3. Deploy as a Web App:

  • 1. Click Deploy (top right) ➔ New deployment.

  • 2. Select Web app as the deployment type (click the gear icon if you don't see it).

  • 3. Fill out the details:

    • Description: Vijigishu Contact Form API
     Execute as: Me (your-gmail-address)
    Who has access: Anyone (This is important so the static site can submit data)
  • 4. Click Deploy and copy the Web App URL generated.

    • #### 4. Connect to your Website:

  • 1. Open contact.html in your editor.

  • 2. Go to the script block at the bottom (around line 439) and paste your copied Google Web App URL into the GOOGLE_SCRIPT_URL variable:

    • const GOOGLE_SCRIPT_URL = 'https://script.google.com/macros/s/.../exec';

    ---

    💡 How it works:


     Once configured, the Javascript form listener will automatically detect the Google Sheets URL.
    Upon submission, the data is pushed directly to your Google Sheet database.
     Your Gmail account automatically triggers a formatted email message sending all lead data directly to your inbox.
    • If you don't want to use Google Sheets, the form defaults back to the Web3Forms key config!

    Session Stage #34 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    how to upload all this site to hostinger server and connect from there to there so that i can push the chagnes accorindly with database and all

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Here is the step-by-step guide to deploying your website to Hostinger and setting up a workflow to push changes continuously, along with handling database/lead storage.

    ---

    🚀 Option 1: Automatic Git Deployment (Recommended for Easy Updates)

    With this setup, whenever you or your team make changes to the code, you simply push to GitHub, and Hostinger will automatically deploy the updates live within seconds.

    #### Step 1: Push Project to GitHub

  • 1. Create a free account at GitHub.com.

  • 2. Create a New Repository (name it e.g. vijigishu-website).

  • 3. In your project directory (C:\Users\Shivam\.gemini\antigravity\scratch\vijigishu-redesign), run these terminal commands:

    • git init
       git add .
       git commit -m "Initial commit of redesigned website"
       git branch -M main
       git remote add origin https://github.com/YOUR_GITHUB_USERNAME/vijigishu-website.git
       git push -u origin main

    #### Step 2: Connect GitHub to Hostinger

  • 1. Log in to your Hostinger hPanel.

  • 2. Go to Websites → Select your domain → Click Manage.

  • 3. Under the Advanced section in the left sidebar, click Git.

  • 4. Fill in the deployment details:

    • Repository: https://github.com/YOUR_GITHUB_USERNAME/vijigishu-website.git
     Branch: main
    Directory: public_html
  • 5. Click Create.

    • #### Step 3: Enable Auto-Deployment (Webhook)

  • 1. Hostinger will generate a Webhook URL.

  • 2. Copy that Webhook URL.

  • 3. In GitHub, go to your repository Settings → Webhooks → Add Webhook.

  • 4. Paste the Hostinger Webhook URL into the Payload URL field and click Add Webhook.

    • ✅ Result: Whenever you make changes and push to GitHub, Hostinger will automatically update your live site!

    ---

    📦 Option 2: Direct File Manager Upload (Quickest 1-Time Setup)

    If you don't want to use Git right away:

  • 1. Open your project folder: C:\Users\Shivam\.gemini\antigravity\scratch\vijigishu-redesign.

  • 2. Select all files (index.htm

    👤 User Request

    how about a private git repo but can i conenct this with the dtabase on the hostinger

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Yes! You can connect a Private Git Repository for automatic deployment and connect a MySQL Database on Hostinger for dynamic features (such as contact forms, student inquiries, program registrations, etc.).

    Here is how both work step-by-step:

    ---

    Part 1: Connecting Private Git Repo to Hostinger (Auto-Deployment)

    Hostinger hPanel has a built-in Git Deployment Tool. Once set up, every time you git push changes to your private GitHub repository, Hostinger automatically updates your live site!

    Step 1: Push Code to a Private GitHub Repository


  • 1. Initialize Git in your project folder (scratch/vijigishu-redesign):

    • git init
       git add .
       git commit -m "Initial commit of Vijigishu website redesign"

  • 2. Create a Private Repository on GitHub.com.

  • 3. Link and push your local code:

    • git remote add origin https://github.com/YOUR_USERNAME/vijigishu-website.git
       git branch -M main
       git push -u origin main

    Step 2: Connect Private GitHub Repo in Hostinger


  • 1. Log in to Hostinger hPanel ➔ Go to Websites ➔ Manage.

  • 2. On the left sidebar under Advanced, click Git.

  • 3. Fill in the details:

    • Repository URL: https://github.com/YOUR_USERNAME/vijigishu-website.git
     Branch: main
    Directory: public_html
  • 4. Copy the SSH Deployment Key generated by Hostinger.

  • 5. Go to your GitHub Repo ➔ Settings ➔ Deploy Keys ➔ Add deploy key ➔ Paste the key and check Allow write access.

  • 6. Click Create in Hostinger to deploy!

    • Step 3: Enable Auto-Deployment (Webhooks)


  • 1. In Hostinger Git settings, copy the Webhook URL.

  • 2. Go to your GitHub Repo ➔ Settings ➔ Webhooks ➔ Add webhook.

  • 3. Paste the URL, set content type to application/json, and save.

  • 4. 🎉 Now, whenever you push code from your computer, your Hostinger site updates automatically!

    • ---

    Part 2: Connecting to a MySQL Database on Hostinger

    Hostinger includes free MySQL Database
    <truncated 208 bytes>
    6789_vijigishu
     Username: e.g., u123456789_user
    Password: Choose a strong password.

  • 3. Click Create.

    • Step 2: Access phpMyAdmin & Create Tables


  • 1. Click Enter phpMyAdmin next to your database.

  • 2. Create tables for storing user submissions (e.g. inquiries table):

    • CREATE TABLE inquiries (
           id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
           name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
           email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
           phone VARCHAR(50),
           program VARCHAR(100),
           message TEXT,
           created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
       );

    Step 3: Connect HTML Forms to Database (e.g. Contact Form)


    Create a     submit-inquiry.php` script in your website folder to process form submissions:

    <?php
    // Hostinger Database Connection Credentials
    $host     = 'localhost'; // Always localhost on Hostinger
    $dbname   = 'u123456789_vijigishu';
    $username = 'u123456789_user';
    $password = 'YOUR_DB_PASSWORD';
    try {
        $pdo = new PDO("mysql:host=$host;dbname=$dbname;charset=utf8", $username, $password, [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION
        ]);
        if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
            $name    = $_POST['name'] ?? '';
            $email   = $_POST['email'] ?? '';
            $phone   = $_POST['phone'] ?? '';
            $message = $_POST['message'] ?? '';
            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO inquiries (name, email, phone, message) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)");
            $stmt->execute([$name, $email, $phone, $message]);
            echo json_encode(['status' => 'success', 'message' => 'Thank you! Your inquiry has been received.']);
        }
    } catch (PDOException $e) {
        echo json_encode(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Database error: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
    }
    ?>

    ---

    💡 Summary of Benefits:


     Private Git Repo: Full version control, privacy, and 1-click automated deployments every time you update your code.
    • Hostinger Database: Secure storage for contact forms, student applications, leads, or CMS integration without extra hosting costs!

    • Let's do a complete analysis of what can be optimized for index.html without changing anything right now, as requested by the user: "do not change anything let me know changes first".

    Let's organize the recommendations into clear, actionable points:

    1. Hero Video Optimization (Biggest Impact — 27MB → ~3MB)


    • Issue: Website-Home-Page-Banner-Video-.mp4 is 27.09 MB, which severely delays initial page load on mobile/slower connections.
    • Solutions:
    • Compress the MP4 video using WebM/H.264 compression (target < 3–4 MB without visible quality loss).
    • Add a lightweight poster image (poster="images/hero-video-poster.jpg") to display instantaneously while the video buffers, preventing black screen flickering.
    • Add preload="none" or preload="metadata" so the video doesn't block critical page render assets.

    2. Image Compression & Resizing (Huge Savings — 1.16MB PNG → ~50KB WebP)


    • Issue: Group-339.png is 1.16 MB.
    • Solutions:
    • Convert PNG images (Group-339.png, logo-of-vijigishu_2-1.png, etc.) to optimized WebP format with 80% quality compression (reduces file size by 80-90%).
    • Compress all -scaled.webp images. Currently they are 600KB - 1MB each; re-saving them at WebP 80% will reduce them to ~100-150KB.

    3. Image Lazy Loading (loading="lazy")


    • Issue: Images below the fold (in Testimonials, Gallery, Partners, Programs) are loaded simultaneously with the hero section.
    • Solution:
    • Add loading="lazy" and decoding="async" to all images below the hero section so they only load when the user scrolls to them.
    • Keep fetchpriority="high" only for above-the-fold images (like logo & hero background).

    4. Font Opt


    <truncated 1722 bytes>
    t block critical page rendering.

    ---

    #### 2. Uncompressed Images (Group-339.png = 1.16 MB)
     Problem: Large PNG and WebP files are unoptimized (e.g. Group-339.png is 1.16 MB, and several -scaled.webp images are 700KB–1MB each).
    Solution:
     Convert Group-339.png to WebP (~60 KB).
    Re-compress all hero/program WebP images to 80% quality, reducing average image size from 800 KB to ~80–120 KB.

    ---

    #### 3. Missing Lazy Loading on Below-the-Fold Images
     Problem: Browsers currently download every image on the entire page immediately on page load, even images at the bottom of the page (Testimonials, Gallery, Partners).
    Solution:
     Add loading="lazy" and decoding="async" to all images below the hero section.
    Keep only the top logo/hero image with fetchpriority="high".

    ---

    #### 4. Google Fonts Preconnect & display=swap
     Problem: Font requests delay text rendering (causes FOIT / invisible text during load).
    Solution:
     Add <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com"> and <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin> to the <head>.

    • Append &display=swap to font URLs so text shows immediately with system fonts while Google Fonts finish loading.

    Session Stage #86 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    asking the top menu and footer menu are global right ? do not change now

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Yes, conceptually the Top Menu and Footer Menu are global components used across all pages of your website.

    Here is how they currently work and how we can make them 100% centralized when hosting on Hostinger:

    ---

    1. How They Currently Work (Static HTML)


    Since the site is built with standard HTML files (index.html, about.html, contact.html, etc.), each page currently contains the <nav id="navbar"> and <footer> HTML blocks.
     Whenever a change is needed (like adding a mascot or removing a menu item), we automatically sync the exact same header and footer across all .html files simultaneously so they stay identical.

    ---

    2. How to Make Them a Single Shared File (Future Hostinger Setup)


    When you host on Hostinger, if you'd like to manage the menu from one single file so any change automatically updates every page:

    PHP Includes (Recommended for Hostinger):
    We can create header.php and footer.php. Then each page simply calls:

    <?php include('header.php'); ?>
      <!-- Page Content -->
      <?php include('footer.php'); ?>

     JavaScript Component (Client-side Include):
    Alternatively, a tiny script can fetch and load navbar.html and footer.html into a <div> on every page automatically.

    ---

    (No changes have been made to your files as requested!) Let me know whenever you're ready or if you have any other questions.

    Session Stage #118 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    Sticky Upload & Add Photo Card: not working please check

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Let's summarize the fix for the user!The Sticky Upload & Add Photo Card in main-admin.html is now working!

    ---

    🔍 Cause & Resolution:


    Why it didn't stick: The parent grid container .gallery-grid-layout previously had align-items: start;, which forced the left column grid cell's height to shrink to the exact height of the form card itself (leaving zero distance for the card to slide and stick).
     The Fix: Removed align-items: start; from .gallery-grid-layout and applied align-self: start; position: sticky; top: 10px; directly to .card-box.sticky-card. Now, as you scroll down through all the gallery photos, the ➕ Upload & Add Photo form stays smoothly pinned to the top of your screen!

    ---

    🌐 Preview:

    Session Stage #120 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    still not wokring please check what is the error not sticekd to the left

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Let's explain the root fix to the user clearly!I have diagnosed and resolved the root cause of why the ➕ Upload & Add Photo box wasn't sticking!

    ---

    🛠️ Root Cause & Solution:


  • 1. Root Cause:

    • Previously, .admin-main-content had overflow-y: auto; set without a strict height: 100vh;.
     In CSS, placing overflow-y: auto on an un-height-constrained parent breaks native position: sticky on all nested children because the browser cannot determine the scroll context boundaries.
  • 2. The Fix:

    • Fixed Sidebar: .admin-sidebar is now position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; bottom: 0; height: 100vh;.
     Window-Level Scrolling: Removed overflow-y: auto from .admin-main-content and enabled natural window browser scrolling (margin-left: 250px).
    Sticky Upload Card: .card-box.sticky-card now binds directly to the primary browser viewport (position: sticky; top: 24px; z-index: 50;).

    ---

    🌐 Verify Live:


  • 1. Open http://127.0.0.1:3000/main-admin.html in your browser.

  • 2. Click 🖼️ Gallery Manager in the left sidebar menu.

  • 3. Scroll down the page—the ➕ Upload & Add Photo card now stays pinned at 24px from the top left of your screen!

    • Committed to Git (main branch).* Open GitHub Desktop and click Push origin to deploy live!

    Session Stage #147 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    what was the issue i want review in short details immersion-programs.html and contact.html

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Here is a short, clear breakdown of what caused the issue on immersion-programs.html and contact.html, and how it was resolved:

    ---

    1. The Root Causes


  • 1. Unclosed <style> Tag in Initial Conversion:

    • When linking css/global-components.css, the </style> tag was missing in the <head> section of those files.
     Browsers treated the entire HTML <body> as CSS rules instead of rendering elements, producing a completely blank white screen.
  • 2. Hidden Content via .reveal Animation:

    • Elements with class .reveal (such as the main contact card and immersion program sections) default to opacity: 0 in CSS.
     If the scroll observer had not fired yet on initial load, those sections remained at opacity: 0 (invisible/white).
  • 3. Conflicting Inline Scripts:

    • Both pages retained old inline event listeners attempting to access #navbar and #hamburger elements before or during dynamic global component injection, causing script execution hiccups.

    ---

    2. How It Was Fixed


  • 1. Clean Head & Closed Tags: Restored proper </style> tags and cleanly loaded css/global-components.css across all pages.

  • 2. Instant Viewport Visibility: Updated js/global-components.js to immediately check bounding boxes on page load and force all visible/above-the-fold .reveal elements to opacity: 1.

  • 3. Cleaned Duplicate Scripts: Removed redundant inline navbar/hamburger scripts so js/global-components.js manages everything smoothly without conflicts.

  • 4. Hero Margin Reset: Ensured the sticky navbar directly touches the top hero image seamlessly without white gaps.

    Design Critique: Is a dark background a good choice for this section?


    Let's evaluate:
    Advantages of a dark background for the "How It Works" timeline:
  • 1. Creates Visual Contrast and Section Separation:

    • In web design, sandwiching sections with alternating light and dark backgrounds (often called "section blocks" or "zebra striping") helps break up visual monotony. Since the sections before #process (Popular Destinations, Academic Disciplines, and Special Learning Tours) all have clean, light backgrounds, a dark background for #process immediately alerts the user's brain: "Here is a change of pace; this is a step-by-step framework."
  • 2. Elevates Brand Premiumness:

    • Generous blocks of the brand's dark navy (var(--navy) / var(--dark)) paired with semi-transparent frosted cards (rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03) with a backdrop-filter: blur(10px)) feel modern, slick, and premium. It mimics modern SaaS/education-consulting aesthetic.
  • 3. Enhances Timeline Visual Weight:

    • A timeline is an informational sequence. Putting it on a dark background forces the user to focus on the illuminated path (the vertical timeline line and glowing color-coded badges) and read through the cards sequentially.

    Disadvantages / Considerations:

  • 1. Contrast Transition:

    • Since the section immediately below is #ip-cta (which is placed in a light container containing a navy card) and the footer below it is dark, we have:
    • Light (Special Learning Tours) -> Dark (How It Works) -> Light (CTA Banner spacing) -> Navy (CTA Card) -> Dark (Footer).


    <truncated 1770 bytes>
    osted glass blur (backdrop-filter) and light borders so they glow softly.
  • 3. Color-Coded Themes:

    • The central vertical line is a custom color-gradient using the brand colors.
    • Each timeline node (circle) and card accent color cycles through the brand's Green, Sage, Gold, and Navy themes.

  • 4. Scroll Reveal Animations:

    • Left cards slide and fade in from the left (translateX(-50px)).
    • Right cards slide and fade in from the right (translateX(50px)).
    • Staggered entry transitions are preserved.

  • 5. Mobile-Responsive Adaptability:

    • On screens under 868px, the timeline vertical line automatically moves to the left edge, and all cards collapse into a unified, left-aligned vertical stack with standard left-side nodes.

    ---

    💬 Design Critique: Is a dark background a good choice here?

    Yes, the dark background is a very good choice. Here is why, considering the page structure and purpose:

    Good Visual Rhythm (Zebra Striping):
    The top sections of the page (Hero Banner, popular destinations, disciplines, and learning tours) all use bright, clean, light backgrounds. If the process section stayed light, the page would feel like a long, endless scroll of white text cards. Injecting a dark block here breaks up the monotony and signals to the user:     "Here is a change of pace; this is a step-by-step guide on how to proceed."
     Aesthetic Focus:
    A dark background makes the color-coded glowing badges, frosted cards, and the central vertical timeline line pop out. It creates a structured, guided path that is easy to digest as the user scrolls.

    • Balanced Page Bottom:

    Since the global footer is dark, having #process dark, followed by the light CTA section (with a navy inner card), and then the dark footer creates a balanced, unified transition at the end of the page rather than an abrupt jump.

    Session Stage #171 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    can you adjust the tranistion to hover a little bit smoother current hover animation is kind of flickring to me. make it smooth

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Awesome! The flickering is now completely resolved.
    The transition is super smooth and looks gorgeous.
    No additional commits or pushes are run, as requested. The reasons for the transitions are clear. I am ready to explain the changes to the user. I will not make any more tool calls.I have resolved the flickering and made all transitions incredibly smooth:

    🛠️ What was causing the flicker:


  • 1. The Layout Jump: The details section used display: none to display: block. Because display properties cannot be animated, the card contents suddenly jumped in height. If your cursor was near the bottom edge when this jump happened, the card elements would instantly shift away from the cursor, triggering a "hover-out" (which hides the text), which then triggers a "hover-in" again. This created an infinite rapid flicker loop.

  • 2. The Background Image: The background image was animating its transform (scale/movement) on hover, but changing its opacity instantly (0s). This caused a brief flash of brightness as the image suddenly dropped opacity.

    • ✨ What I changed to make it smooth:


    Fluid max-height & opacity transitions: I replaced the display: none toggle on .slide-out-details with a combination of max-height: 0 / opacity: 0 and max-height: 240px / opacity: 1. It now grows fluidly and fades in over 0.45s using a premium easing curve (cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)).
     Fading Background Image: Added opacity to the .slide-out-image transition rules (transition: transform 0.6s var(--ease), opacity 0.6s var(--ease)). The background image now dims out as a slow, soft transition instead of flashing.
    • Fading Icon and Text Colors: Standardized the icon's hover transitions (border-color and color) to fade rather than snap.

    All changes are local and ready for you to verify on your browser before committing:

    🛠️ How to Fix & Deploy on Hostinger


  • 1. Log in to Hostinger hPanel:

    • Go to Websites &rarr; Select pre-prod.vijigishu.co.in &rarr; Manage.

  • 3. Enable Auto-Deployment (Recommended):

    • On the same Hostinger Git page, copy the Webhook URL.
    • Go to your GitHub repo &rarr; Settings &rarr; Webhooks &rarr; Add Webhook &rarr; Paste the URL and save.

  • 4. Purge Cache:

    • Go to Performance / Cache Manager in hPanel and click Purge All to immediately clear the CDN cache.

    2. Zero-Downtime Migration Plan: pre-prod &rarr; vijigishu.co.in

    Because the codebase already uses root-relative links (href="about", href="contact"), standard assets (images/...), and canonical tags already preset to https://vijigishu.co.in/, no code refactoring is needed when switching domains.

    #### Step 1: Point Git Deployment to Main Domain in Hostinger

  • 1. Log in to Hostinger hPanel &rarr; Websites &rarr; Select vijigishu.co.in &rarr; Manage.

  • 2. Go to Advanced &rarr; Git.

  • 3. Link the repository:

    • Repository: https://github.com/Shivam1101/vijigishu-website.git
    • Branch: main
    • Install Directory: /public_html

  • 4. Click Deploy.

    • #### Step 2: Add GitHub Webhook for Automatic Instant Deploys

  • 1. Copy the Webhook URL generated by Hostinger in the Git section.

  • 2. In GitHub (Shivam1101/vijigishu-website), go to Settings &rarr; Webhooks &rarr; Add Webhook.

  • 3. Paste the URL, set Content-Type to application/json, and select Just the push event.

    • #### Step 3: Verify SSL & HTTPS

  • 1. In Hostinger hPanel under vijigishu.co.in, go to Security &rarr; SSL.

  • 2. Ensure Lifetime SSL is active and toggle Force HTTPS. (Our .htaccess also enforces HTTPS automatically).

    • #### Step 4: DNS / Cache Flush

  • 1. In hPanel, go to Performance &rarr; Cache Manager &rarr; Click Purge All.

  • 2. If pre-prod.vijigishu.co.in should redirect to the main site:

    • Add a 301 domain redirect in hPanel &rarr; Redirects: https://pre-prod.vijigishu.co.in/ &rarr; https://vijigishu.co.in/$1.

    #### Step 5: Verification Checklist

    • [x] Test clean URLs (e.g. https://vijigishu.co.in/about) load seamlessly without .html.
    • [x] Test broken URLs (e.g. https://vijigishu.co.in/random-page) load the custom 404 page.
    • [x] Submit https://vijigishu.co.in/sitemap.xml to Google Search Console.

    Session Stage #301 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    1 task running

    npx -y serve -l 3000 .

    is this counter under usage ?

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    No, it does not count toward your AI usage or token quota.

    • npx -y serve -l 3000 . runs entirely on your local machine as a lightweight local web server to let you preview http://localhost:3000.
    • Your usage quota is only consumed when sending messages/prompts and generating AI responses. Keeping the server running in the background is completely free of usage limits.

    Here are 3 clean, uncluttered design ideas to switch between disciplines (Architecture, Engineering, School Programs) from the Management page:

    ---

    1. Floating Glassmorphic Bottom Pill Dock (Dynamic Island Style)


    Placement: Centered floating at the bottom of the screen (or bottom-left, ~24px above edge).
     Look & Feel: Frosted luxury dark-navy pill with a subtle sage border (backdrop-filter: blur(16px); border-radius: 100px;).
    How it Works:
    • Displays a small label "Other Disciplines:" followed by 3 sleek pill chips: 🏛️ Architecture, ⚙️ Engineering, 🎓 School Programs.
    • Current discipline (Management) is subtly indicated or omitted.
    • Sits out of the way of reading content; can auto-minimize to a single dot when scrolling down and re-expand when scrolling stops.

    ---

    2. Minimalist Vertical Edge Rail (Subtle Side Dock)


     Placement: Fixed vertically centered on the right screen edge.
    Look & Feel: Ultra-thin, 44px-wide vertical glass strip with 4 subtle icons: 💼 (Management - Active), 📐 (Architecture), ⚙️ (Engineering), 🎓 (School Programs).
     How it Works:
    • In default state, it takes virtually zero screen real estate.
    • Hovering over any icon smoothly slides out an elegant label card ("Explore Architecture & Design →") with 1-click navigation.

    ---

    3. Discipline Switcher Speed Dial FAB (Pop-out Action Button)


    Placement: Fixed bottom-left (balanced opposite the bottom-right Go-to-Top compass).
     Look & Feel: Single elegant circular button with a layered icon: Switch Discipline ▾.
    • How it Works:
    • Clicking/hovering triggers a staggered vertical stack of 3 compact cards with program thumbnail icons and direct links to Architecture, Engineering, and School Programs.
    • 100% clutter-free since options appear only when the user wants to switch.

    ---

    Which approach do you prefer to implement?


  • 1. Idea 1 (Bottom Glassmorphic Pill Dock)

  • 3. Idea 3 (Speed Dial FAB)

    Session Stage #322 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    just asking do not implement this -- how much of effort making the dark and ligh mode switch for all the pages.

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Effort Estimate: Low to Moderate (~1.5 – 2 hours total implementation)

    Because the website is already structured with a centralized header/footer component (global-components.js & css/global-components.css) and standardized CSS variables, adding a site-wide Dark/Light mode is quite straightforward.

    ---

    Key Steps Involved:


  • 1. Centralized CSS Variable Overrides (~30 mins)

    • Define a [data-theme="dark"] attribute in global-components.css:
    • --white: #0D0E2E (or #121338)
    • --off: #181944 (dark card surfaces)
    • --navy: #FFFFFF / #ECEEF8 (text/headings)
    • --gray: #A2A4BA (subtext)
    • Adjust card border transparencies and drop-shadows for dark backgrounds.

  • 2. Global Toggle Switch Component (~20 mins)

    • Add a subtle Sun/Moon switch button directly into global-components.js navbar.
    • Saves user preference to localStorage and automatically syncs with system OS (prefers-color-scheme).

  • 3. Page-by-Page Polish & Contrast Testing (~45 mins)

    • Audit all 25+ pages for any hardcoded background: #FFFFFF or color: #1B1C4A styles to ensure they reference CSS variables.
    • Check contrast on accordions, itinerary boxes, glance cards, and footer badges.

    ---

    Summary:


    • Feasibility: Very high & clean to implement without touching backend logic.
    • Risks/Trade-offs: Low; all changes can be controlled seamlessly via [data-theme="dark"] without breaking light mode.

    Session Stage #334 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    for the mobile view the aboutus and leadership walk and contact us are got mapped under the immersion program

    update this issue and also check fro the logo it opens some page and not on some pages .

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    Root Cause & Detailed Explanation:


  • 1. Why About Us, Leadership Walk & Contact Us got mapped under /immersion-programs/:

    • Reason: On inner immersion pages (e.g. immersion-programs/management.html, immersion-programs/japan.html, etc.), several internal CTA buttons and breadcrumbs had relative paths like href="contact", href="about.html", or href="contact.html?type=..." without a leading root slash (/).
    • When clicked from inside /immersion-programs/, the browser naturally resolved them relative to the current subfolder as https://domain.com/immersion-programs/contact and https://domain.com/immersion-programs/about.

  • 2. Why the Logo opened on some pages and not on others:

    • Reason: The header logo link in global-components.js previously evaluated to / universally. When browsing via local directory previews or subpath staging without root URL rewrites, href="/" tried to navigate to root file directories or port roots (file:/// or subpath origins) rather than the project homepage index.html.

    ---

    What Was Fixed:


    • Root-Absolute Navigation Mapping: Updated js/global-components.js with a universal environment resolver (/, /about, /immersion-programs, /leadership-walk, /gallery, /contact) that routes to the correct main pages from any nesting depth.
    • Fixed All 16 Immersion Inner Pages: Replaced all relative contact, about, and enquiry bell links inside immersion-programs/ with /contact, /about, and /leadership-walk.
    • Resilient Logo Link: The logo now navigates to the homepage regardless of whether the site is accessed via standard web server, pre-prod domain, or offline preview.

    Changes committed and pushed to GitHub main.

    Session Stage #344 Vijigishu Web Project
    👤 User Request

    why there is immersion-programs/index.html page?

    🤖 Antigravity AI Assistant

    immersion-programs/index.html exists for clean URL and web server directory routing:

  • 1. Directory URL Resolution: When a user or search engine visits /immersion-programs/ (as a directory with a trailing slash), web servers (Nginx, Apache, GitHub Pages, Netlify, Vercel) automatically serve index.html inside that folder.

  • 2. Preventing 404 / 403 Errors: Without immersion-programs/index.html, opening https://domain.com/immersion-programs/ would result in a 404 Not Found or 403 Directory Listing Denied error on many hosting servers.

  • 3. Seamless Routing: Having both immersion-programs.html and immersion-programs/index.html ensures the page loads cleanly whether accessed with or without a trailing slash (/immersion-programs vs /immersion-programs/).
